### 词群 19.01｜人的时代位置与身份信息 {#group-19-01}

**串联方式：** 从先驱、预言者和传承者进入世代、姓氏与性别；身份词与能力评价分开。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-001">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-001" class="vocabulary-word-title">pioneer<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-001" aria-label="定位 pioneer">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-001" data-mark-label="pioneer" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="pioneer：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="pioneer：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pioneer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 pioneer" title="在有道查询 pioneer">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-001</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.先驱，开创者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historian described the reformer as a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pioneer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pioneer</strong></a>, a prophet of social change and a herald of a new era.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史学家将改革者称为先驱、社会变革的预言者和新时代的先声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike a religious prophet, the scientific <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pioneer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pioneer</strong></a> became a herald of change through experiments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与宗教先知不同，这位科学先驱通过实验成为变革的先声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition asks whether the inventor was a practical <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=pioneer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>pioneer</strong></a>, a visionary prophet or a herald of industrial progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览探讨这位发明家是务实先驱、有远见的预言者，还是工业进步的先声。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a medical pioneer</span> · 医学先驱</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=innovation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">innovation</a></span> · 创新</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=visionary&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">visionary</a></span> · 有远见者</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-002">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-002" class="vocabulary-word-title">prophet<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-002" aria-label="定位 prophet">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-002" data-mark-label="prophet" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="prophet：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="prophet：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prophet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 prophet" title="在有道查询 prophet">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-002</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (基督教、犹太教、伊斯兰教的)先知；预言家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historian described the reformer as a pioneer, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prophet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prophet</strong></a> of social change and a herald of a new era.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史学家将改革者称为先驱、社会变革的预言者和新时代的先声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike a religious <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prophet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prophet</strong></a>, the scientific pioneer became a herald of change through experiments.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与宗教先知不同，这位科学先驱通过实验成为变革的先声。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition asks whether the inventor was a practical pioneer, a visionary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=prophet&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>prophet</strong></a> or a herald of industrial progress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览探讨这位发明家是务实先驱、有远见的预言者，还是工业进步的先声。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a religious prophet</span> · 宗教先知</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-004">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-004" class="vocabulary-word-title">heir<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-004" aria-label="定位 heir">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-004" data-mark-label="heir" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="heir：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="heir：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 heir" title="在有道查询 heir">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-004</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.后嗣；继承人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heir</strong></a> became her father's successor and represented a new generation of leaders.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位继承人接替父亲，代表了新一代领导者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each generation expected an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heir</strong></a> to become the family firm's successor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每一代都期待一位继承人成为家族企业的接班人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than appoint an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=heir&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>heir</strong></a> automatically, the board chose a successor from a younger generation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会没有自动任命继承人，而是从年轻一代中选出接班人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an heir to the estate</span> · 遗产继承人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 继承</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">succession</a></span> · 接任</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-005">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-005" class="vocabulary-word-title">successor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-005" aria-label="定位 successor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-005" data-mark-label="successor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="successor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="successor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=successor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 successor" title="在有道查询 successor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-005</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.接替者，继任者；后继的事物</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The heir became her father's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=successor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>successor</strong></a> and represented a new generation of leaders.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位继承人接替父亲，代表了新一代领导者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each generation expected an heir to become the family firm's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=successor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>successor</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每一代都期待一位继承人成为家族企业的接班人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Rather than appoint an heir automatically, the board chose a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=successor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>successor</strong></a> from a younger generation.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">董事会没有自动任命继承人，而是从年轻一代中选出接班人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a political successor</span> · 政治接班人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=inheritance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">inheritance</a></span> · 继承</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=succession&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">succession</a></span> · 接任</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-007">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-007" class="vocabulary-word-title">surname<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-007" aria-label="定位 surname">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-007" data-mark-label="surname" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="surname：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="surname：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surname&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 surname" title="在有道查询 surname">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-007</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.姓；v.给······起别名</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The form asks for a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surname&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surname</strong></a> and recorded sex, while an optional section lets a female applicant describe her gender identity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">表格要求填写姓氏和登记性别，另有可选部分供女性申请者描述性别认同。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Researchers removed each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=surname&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>surname</strong></a> before analysing sex and gender data, including responses from female participants.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">研究者分析生理性别和社会性别数据前，删除了各人姓氏，包含女性参与者的答复。</p></li>

### 词群 19.02｜亲属、伴侣与亲密称呼 {#group-19-02}

**串联方式：** 先认伴侣和亲属关系，再表达亲近与爱意；gay 是性取向相关词，不等同亲属称谓。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-011">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-011" class="vocabulary-word-title">couple<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-011" aria-label="定位 couple">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-011" data-mark-label="couple" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="couple：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="couple：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=couple&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 couple" title="在有道查询 couple">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-011</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.夫妻，情侣；两个，几个</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The gay <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=couple&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>couple</strong></a> moved nearby, and one spouse introduced his husband to the neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这对同性伴侣搬到附近，一方把丈夫介绍给邻居。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The article follows a gay <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=couple&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>couple</strong></a> as each spouse balances work and caring for his husband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章跟随一对同性伴侣，描写双方如何平衡工作与照顾丈夫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the event, a gay <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=couple&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>couple</strong></a> explained how the word spouse applies equally to each husband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">活动中，一对同性伴侣解释配偶一词同样适用于两位丈夫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a married couple</span> · 已婚伴侣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marriage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marriage</a></span> · 婚姻</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=partnership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">partnership</a></span> · 伴侣关系</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-012">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-012" class="vocabulary-word-title">spouse<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-012" aria-label="定位 spouse">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-012" data-mark-label="spouse" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="spouse：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="spouse：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 spouse" title="在有道查询 spouse">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-012</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The article follows a gay couple as each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spouse</strong></a> balances work and caring for his husband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章跟随一对同性伴侣，描写双方如何平衡工作与照顾丈夫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the event, a gay couple explained how the word <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=spouse&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>spouse</strong></a> applies equally to each husband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">活动中，一对同性伴侣解释配偶一词同样适用于两位丈夫。</p></li>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gay</strong></a> couple moved nearby, and one spouse introduced his husband to the neighbours.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这对同性伴侣搬到附近，一方把丈夫介绍给邻居。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The article follows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gay</strong></a> couple as each spouse balances work and caring for his husband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">文章跟随一对同性伴侣，描写双方如何平衡工作与照顾丈夫。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">At the event, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=gay&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>gay</strong></a> couple explained how the word spouse applies equally to each husband.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">活动中，一对同性伴侣解释配偶一词同样适用于两位丈夫。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a gay couple</span> · 同性伴侣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marriage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marriage</a></span> · 婚姻</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=partnership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">partnership</a></span> · 伴侣关系</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-015">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-015" class="vocabulary-word-title">grandfather<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-015" aria-label="定位 grandfather">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-015" data-mark-label="grandfather" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="grandfather：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="grandfather：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grandfather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 grandfather" title="在有道查询 grandfather">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-015</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (外)祖父</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">My <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grandfather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grandfather</strong></a> invited my father-in-law, my sibling and a cousin to dinner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我祖父请我的岳父、兄弟姐妹和一位表亲吃饭。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A family photograph shows her <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grandfather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grandfather</strong></a> beside her father-in-law, with a sibling and a cousin behind them.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家庭照中，她的祖父站在公公旁边，一位手足与一位表亲站在后面。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The reunion helped a cousin meet my <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=grandfather&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>grandfather</strong></a>, my father-in-law and my youngest sibling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这次团聚让一位表亲见到了我的祖父、岳父和最小的弟妹。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a maternal grandfather</span> · 外祖父</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=family%20reunion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">family reunion</a></span> · 家庭团聚</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=extended%20family&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">extended family</a></span> · 大家庭</li></ul>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.侄子；外甥</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">My <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nephew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nephew</strong></a> and niece came with a twin cousin, while another relative helped us contact their next of kin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我的侄子和侄女与一位双胞胎表亲到来，另一位亲属帮我们联系其最近亲属。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family register lists each relative, including my <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nephew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nephew</strong></a>, niece and twin brother, alongside their next of kin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家族登记册列出各位亲属，包括我的侄子、侄女和双胞胎兄弟，并注明其最近亲属。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A relative cared for the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=nephew&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>nephew</strong></a> and niece while their twin uncles searched for the next of kin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位亲属照顾侄子和侄女，同时他们的双胞胎叔叔寻找最近亲属。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a young nephew</span> · 年幼的侄子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guardianship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">guardianship</a></span> · 监护</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=family%20register&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">family register</a></span> · 家族登记册</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">My nephew and niece came with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twin</strong></a> cousin, while another relative helped us contact their next of kin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我的侄子和侄女与一位双胞胎表亲到来，另一位亲属帮我们联系其最近亲属。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The family register lists each relative, including my nephew, niece and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twin</strong></a> brother, alongside their next of kin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">家族登记册列出各位亲属，包括我的侄子、侄女和双胞胎兄弟，并注明其最近亲属。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A relative cared for the nephew and niece while their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=twin&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>twin</strong></a> uncles searched for the next of kin.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位亲属照顾侄子和侄女，同时他们的双胞胎叔叔寻找最近亲属。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an identical twin</span> · 同卵双胞胎</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=guardianship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">guardianship</a></span> · 监护</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=family%20register&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">family register</a></span> · 家族登记册</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-034">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-034" class="vocabulary-word-title">intimate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-034" aria-label="定位 intimate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-034" data-mark-label="intimate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="intimate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="intimate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 intimate" title="在有道查询 intimate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-034</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.亲密的；个人隐私的；n.密友，至交</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the song, a lover calls his beloved darling and tells a dear friend about their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intimate</strong></a> bond.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">歌中，恋人称挚爱为亲爱的，并向亲密朋友讲述他们的亲密关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The poet's beloved lover treasured an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intimate</strong></a> letter that began with dear and ended with darling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">诗人的挚爱恋人珍藏一封以“亲爱的”开头、以“宝贝”结尾的亲密信件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel ends when a lover finds his beloved darling and shares the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=intimate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>intimate</strong></a> news with a dear friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说结尾，恋人找到心爱的宝贝，并将私密消息告诉一位亲密朋友。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an intimate relationship</span> · 亲密关系</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endearment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">endearment</a></span> · 爱称</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">affection</a></span> · 感情</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The poet's beloved lover treasured an intimate letter that began with <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dear&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dear</strong></a> and ended with darling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">诗人的挚爱恋人珍藏一封以“亲爱的”开头、以“宝贝”结尾的亲密信件。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the song, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lover</strong></a> calls his beloved darling and tells a dear friend about their intimate bond.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">歌中，恋人称挚爱为亲爱的，并向亲密朋友讲述他们的亲密关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The poet's beloved <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lover</strong></a> treasured an intimate letter that began with dear and ended with darling.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">诗人的挚爱恋人珍藏一封以“亲爱的”开头、以“宝贝”结尾的亲密信件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel ends when a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=lover&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>lover</strong></a> finds his beloved darling and shares the intimate news with a dear friend.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说结尾，恋人找到心爱的宝贝，并将私密消息告诉一位亲密朋友。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a former lover</span> · 前恋人</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=endearment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">endearment</a></span> · 爱称</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">affection</a></span> · 感情</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

### 词群 19.03｜生命早期与青少年阶段 {#group-19-03}

**串联方式：** 由胚胎、婴儿到少年、青年和青春期；orphan 指家庭处境，gang 指群体。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-022">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-022" class="vocabulary-word-title">embryo<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-022" aria-label="定位 embryo">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-022" data-mark-label="embryo" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="embryo：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="embryo：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embryo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 embryo" title="在有道查询 embryo">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-022</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.胚胎；adj.胚胎的；初期的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The documentary follows an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embryo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embryo</strong></a> through birth to infancy, then tells the story of an infant who became an orphan.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">纪录片追踪胚胎从出生到婴儿期，随后讲述一个成为孤儿的婴儿的故事。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charity supports <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embryo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embryo</strong></a> research and infant care, and also funds services for every orphan in the region.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该慈善机构支持胚胎研究和婴儿护理，也资助当地每位孤儿获得服务。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition separates <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=embryo&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>embryo</strong></a> development from infant welfare and the social support an orphan may need.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览将胚胎发育与婴儿福利，以及孤儿可能需要的社会支持分开介绍。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a developing embryo</span> · 发育中的胚胎</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=development&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">development</a></span> · 发育</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=child%20welfare&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">child welfare</a></span> · 儿童福利</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The charity supports embryo research and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=infant&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>infant</strong></a> care, and also funds services for every orphan in the region.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该慈善机构支持胚胎研究和婴儿护理，也资助当地每位孤儿获得服务。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Youth services help a vulnerable youngster avoid entering the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=juvenile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>juvenile</strong></a> justice system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">青少年服务帮助弱势孩子避免进入少年司法体系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The youth worker explained <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=juvenile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>juvenile</strong></a> rights to each youngster at the centre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">青少年工作者向中心的每个孩子解释未成年人的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A youngster in the film attends a youth programme designed to reduce <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=juvenile&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>juvenile</strong></a> offending.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影中的孩子参加了旨在减少少年犯罪的青少年项目。</p></li>
</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Youth services help a vulnerable <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=youngster&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>youngster</strong></a> avoid entering the juvenile justice system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">青少年服务帮助弱势孩子避免进入少年司法体系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The youth worker explained juvenile rights to each <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=youngster&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>youngster</strong></a> at the centre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">青少年工作者向中心的每个孩子解释未成年人的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=youngster&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>youngster</strong></a> in the film attends a youth programme designed to reduce juvenile offending.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影中的孩子参加了旨在减少少年犯罪的青少年项目。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=youth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>Youth</strong></a> services help a vulnerable youngster avoid entering the juvenile justice system.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">青少年服务帮助弱势孩子避免进入少年司法体系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=youth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>youth</strong></a> worker explained juvenile rights to each youngster at the centre.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">青少年工作者向中心的每个孩子解释未成年人的权利。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A youngster in the film attends a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=youth&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>youth</strong></a> programme designed to reduce juvenile offending.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">电影中的孩子参加了旨在减少少年犯罪的青少年项目。</p></li>

### 词群 19.04｜家庭角色与家务 {#group-19-04}

**串联方式：** 比较称谓、主人、房东及家庭身份，再看家庭与家务；mistress 等词须辨具体语境。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-039">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-039" class="vocabulary-word-title">madam<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-039" aria-label="定位 madam">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-039" data-mark-label="madam" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="madam：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="madam：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=madam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 madam" title="在有道查询 madam">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-039</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.夫人，支士；［英］自以为是的小姑娘</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The hostess said, 'Welcome, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=madam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>madam</strong></a>,' as the landlady arrived for dinner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">女房东来吃晚饭时，女主人说：“欢迎，女士。”</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When the landlady asked for her coat, the hostess replied, 'Certainly, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=madam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>madam</strong></a>.'</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">女房东要她的大衣时，女主人答道：“当然，女士。”</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The hostess politely addressed the new landlady as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=madam&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>madam</strong></a> before introducing the other guests.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">女主人先礼貌地称新房东为女士，然后介绍其他宾客。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">yes, madam</span> · 好的，女士</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hospitality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hospitality</a></span> · 待客之道</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=politeness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">politeness</a></span> · 礼貌</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-040">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostess</strong></a> said, 'Welcome, madam,' as the landlady arrived for dinner.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">女房东来吃晚饭时，女主人说：“欢迎，女士。”</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When the landlady asked for her coat, the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostess</strong></a> replied, 'Certainly, madam.'</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">女房东要她的大衣时，女主人答道：“当然，女士。”</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostess&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostess</strong></a> politely addressed the new landlady as madam before introducing the other guests.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">女主人先礼貌地称新房东为女士，然后介绍其他宾客。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">When the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=landlady&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>landlady</strong></a> asked for her coat, the hostess replied, 'Certainly, madam.'</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">女房东要她的大衣时，女主人答道：“当然，女士。”</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.女主人；情妇</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The widow became <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mistress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mistress</strong></a> of the house after years as a housewife, and hired a maid to help.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位寡妇当了多年家庭主妇后成为宅邸女主人，并雇了一位女佣帮忙。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The diary contrasts a maid's duties with the life of a housewife and a wealthy widow who was <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mistress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mistress</strong></a> of a large estate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">日记比较了女佣职责、主妇生活，以及作为大庄园女主人的富裕寡妇的生活。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">In the novel, a maid supports a widow adjusting to life as <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mistress&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mistress</strong></a> of a household after being a housewife.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中，一位女佣支持寡妇适应由家庭主妇转为一家女主人的生活。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 19.05｜从接待到约见与陪伴 {#group-19-05}

**串联方式：** 用主人、房东、租客、客人和客户组成服务场景，再进入相遇、约定与陪伴；delegate 与 coward 另辨角色和评价。

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The landlord will <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=host&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>host</strong></a> a dinner for the new tenant and a guest.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">房东将为新租客及一位客人举办晚宴。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=host&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>host</strong></a>, the tenant welcomed the landlord and another guest into the flat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">作为主人，租客欢迎房东和另一位客人来到公寓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The guest thanked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=host&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>host</strong></a>, a former tenant who now rented the house from a different landlord.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">客人感谢主人；主人曾是租客，现在从另一位房东那里租下了这座房子。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">host a dinner</span> · 举办晚宴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=tenancy&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">tenancy</a></span> · 租赁关系</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hospitality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">hospitality</a></span> · 待客之道</li></ul>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An encounter with an unhappy <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>customer</strong></a> taught the consultant how to support a client.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与一位不满顾客的相遇，让顾问学会了如何支持客户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report compares a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>customer</strong></a>'s brief encounter in a shop with a client's long-term relationship with an adviser.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告比较顾客在商店的短暂接触与客户同顾问的长期关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A positive encounter can turn a first-time <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=customer&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>customer</strong></a> into a regular client.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次积极接触能把首次消费的顾客变成常客。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">customer service</span> · 客户服务</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=service%20quality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">service quality</a></span> · 服务质量</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=relationship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">relationship</a></span> · 关系</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report compares a customer's brief encounter in a shop with a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=client&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>client</strong></a>'s long-term relationship with an adviser.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告比较顾客在商店的短暂接触与客户同顾问的长期关系。</p></li>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">An <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=encounter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>encounter</strong></a> with an unhappy customer taught the consultant how to support a client.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与一位不满顾客的相遇，让顾问学会了如何支持客户。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The report compares a customer's brief <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=encounter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>encounter</strong></a> in a shop with a client's long-term relationship with an adviser.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">报告比较顾客在商店的短暂接触与客户同顾问的长期关系。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A positive <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=encounter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>encounter</strong></a> can turn a first-time customer into a regular client.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一次积极接触能把首次消费的顾客变成常客。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-056">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-056" class="vocabulary-word-title">chase<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-056" aria-label="定位 chase">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-056" data-mark-label="chase" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="chase：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="chase：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 chase" title="在有道查询 chase">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-056</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.追逐；追求；n.追逐；追求</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">I had to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chase</strong></a> the bus to accompany my father and avoid missing the appointment, though he warned me not to miss my own meeting.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">我不得不追公交车来陪父亲赴约，以免错过预约，尽管他提醒我别错过自己的会议。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">If you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chase</strong></a> every opportunity, you may miss the appointment you promised to accompany your friend to.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">如果你追逐每个机会，可能会错过答应陪朋友去的预约。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">She offered to accompany me to the appointment so I would not <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chase&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chase</strong></a> the wrong bus and miss the interview.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">她提出陪我赴约，以免我追错公交车而错过面试。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">chase a bus</span> · 追公交车</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=punctuality&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">punctuality</a></span> · 守时</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=companionship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">companionship</a></span> · 陪伴</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-060">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-060" class="vocabulary-word-title">dependent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-060" aria-label="定位 dependent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-060" data-mark-label="dependent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="dependent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="dependent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dependent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 dependent" title="在有道查询 dependent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-060</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.依靠的，依赖的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The delegate defended a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dependent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dependent</strong></a> child and refused to let anyone call the frightened boy a coward.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表保护了一名受抚养的孩子，不允许任何人称这个受惊的男孩为胆小鬼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dependent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dependent</strong></a> family member praised the delegate for speaking honestly when others dismissed him as a coward.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位受其供养的家人赞扬代表诚实发言，即使别人把他斥为胆小鬼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel's delegate feels like a coward when his <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dependent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>dependent</strong></a> relatives need help he cannot provide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中的代表无法提供受其供养亲属所需的帮助，因此觉得自己懦弱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a dependent child</span> · 受抚养的孩子</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=responsibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">responsibility</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=courage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">courage</a></span> · 勇气</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-061">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-061" class="vocabulary-word-title">delegate<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-061" aria-label="定位 delegate">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-061" data-mark-label="delegate" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="delegate：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="delegate：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 delegate" title="在有道查询 delegate">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-061</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.代表；v.授权，委派</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delegate</strong></a> defended a dependent child and refused to let anyone call the frightened boy a coward.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">代表保护了一名受抚养的孩子，不允许任何人称这个受惊的男孩为胆小鬼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A dependent family member praised the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delegate</strong></a> for speaking honestly when others dismissed him as a coward.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位受其供养的家人赞扬代表诚实发言，即使别人把他斥为胆小鬼。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel's <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=delegate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>delegate</strong></a> feels like a coward when his dependent relatives need help he cannot provide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中的代表无法提供受其供养亲属所需的帮助，因此觉得自己懦弱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a conference delegate</span> · 会议代表</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=responsibility&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">responsibility</a></span> · 责任</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=courage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">courage</a></span> · 勇气</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-062">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-062" class="vocabulary-word-title">coward<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-062" aria-label="定位 coward">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-062" data-mark-label="coward" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="coward：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="coward：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coward&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 coward" title="在有道查询 coward">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-062</span></span></summary>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel's delegate feels like a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=coward&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>coward</strong></a> when his dependent relatives need help he cannot provide.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说中的代表无法提供受其供养亲属所需的帮助，因此觉得自己懦弱。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 19.06｜关系怎样建立和变化 {#group-19-06}

**串联方式：** 从求婚、订婚到结婚、婚礼、蜜月与离婚，再回到单身、各自和个人。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-063">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He planned to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propose</strong></a> in June, announce the engagement in July and marry in spring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他计划六月求婚、七月宣布订婚，并在春天结婚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propose</strong></a>, discuss when to marry and how long the engagement should last.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">求婚前，应讨论何时结婚，以及订婚期应该多久。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The couple chose to marry after a short engagement, although neither had expected the other to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=propose&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>propose</strong></a> so soon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这对伴侣决定在短暂订婚后结婚，尽管双方都未料到对方会如此快求婚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">propose marriage</span> · 求婚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commitment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commitment</a></span> · 承诺</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marriage%20plans&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marriage plans</a></span> · 婚姻计划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-064">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-064" class="vocabulary-word-title">engagement<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-064" aria-label="定位 engagement">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-064" data-mark-label="engagement" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="engagement：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="engagement：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 engagement" title="在有道查询 engagement">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-064</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.婚约；约定</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He planned to propose in June, announce the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>engagement</strong></a> in July and marry in spring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他计划六月求婚、七月宣布订婚，并在春天结婚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you propose, discuss when to marry and how long the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>engagement</strong></a> should last.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">求婚前，应讨论何时结婚，以及订婚期应该多久。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The couple chose to marry after a short <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=engagement&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>engagement</strong></a>, although neither had expected the other to propose so soon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这对伴侣决定在短暂订婚后结婚，尽管双方都未料到对方会如此快求婚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">announce an engagement</span> · 宣布订婚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commitment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commitment</a></span> · 承诺</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marriage%20plans&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marriage plans</a></span> · 婚姻计划</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-065">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-065" class="vocabulary-word-title">marry<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-065" aria-label="定位 marry">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-065" data-mark-label="marry" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="marry：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="marry：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 marry" title="在有道查询 marry">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-065</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.娶，嫁，结婚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">He planned to propose in June, announce the engagement in July and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marry</strong></a> in spring.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他计划六月求婚、七月宣布订婚，并在春天结婚。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Before you propose, discuss when to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marry</strong></a> and how long the engagement should last.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">求婚前，应讨论何时结婚，以及订婚期应该多久。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The couple chose to <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marry&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>marry</strong></a> after a short engagement, although neither had expected the other to propose so soon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这对伴侣决定在短暂订婚后结婚，尽管双方都未料到对方会如此快求婚。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">marry a partner</span> · 与伴侣结婚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commitment&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">commitment</a></span> · 承诺</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=marriage%20plans&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">marriage plans</a></span> · 婚姻计划</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-066">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-066" class="vocabulary-word-title">wedding<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-066" aria-label="定位 wedding">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-066" data-mark-label="wedding" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="wedding：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="wedding：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wedding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 wedding" title="在有道查询 wedding">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-066</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.婚礼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wedding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wedding</strong></a> and a final kiss goodbye, the couple left for their honeymoon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">婚礼后，伴侣们最后相吻告别，随后动身度蜜月。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The photograph captures a kiss at the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wedding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wedding</strong></a> before the honeymoon began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">照片捕捉了蜜月开始前婚礼上的一个吻。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They remembered the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=wedding&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>wedding</strong></a>, their first married kiss and the quiet honeymoon by the sea.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们回忆婚礼、婚后的第一吻和海边安静的蜜月。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a wedding ceremony</span> · 婚礼</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">celebration</a></span> · 庆祝</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=newlyweds&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">newlyweds</a></span> · 新婚伴侣</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-067">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-067" class="vocabulary-word-title">honeymoon<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-067" aria-label="定位 honeymoon">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-067" data-mark-label="honeymoon" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="honeymoon：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="honeymoon：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honeymoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 honeymoon" title="在有道查询 honeymoon">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-067</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.蜜月</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the wedding and a final kiss goodbye, the couple left for their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honeymoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honeymoon</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">婚礼后，伴侣们最后相吻告别，随后动身度蜜月。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The photograph captures a kiss at the wedding before the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honeymoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honeymoon</strong></a> began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">照片捕捉了蜜月开始前婚礼上的一个吻。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They remembered the wedding, their first married kiss and the quiet <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=honeymoon&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>honeymoon</strong></a> by the sea.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们回忆婚礼、婚后的第一吻和海边安静的蜜月。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a honeymoon trip</span> · 蜜月旅行</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=celebration&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">celebration</a></span> · 庆祝</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=newlyweds&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">newlyweds</a></span> · 新婚伴侣</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-068">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-068" class="vocabulary-word-title">kiss<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-068" aria-label="定位 kiss">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-068" data-mark-label="kiss" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="kiss：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="kiss：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 kiss" title="在有道查询 kiss">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-068</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">v.吻，接吻；n.吻</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the wedding and a final <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kiss</strong></a> goodbye, the couple left for their honeymoon.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">婚礼后，伴侣们最后相吻告别，随后动身度蜜月。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The photograph captures a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kiss</strong></a> at the wedding before the honeymoon began.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">照片捕捉了蜜月开始前婚礼上的一个吻。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">They remembered the wedding, their first married <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kiss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>kiss</strong></a> and the quiet honeymoon by the sea.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">他们回忆婚礼、婚后的第一吻和海边安静的蜜月。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a farewell kiss</span> · 告别吻</p>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-069">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-069" class="vocabulary-word-title">divorce<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-069" aria-label="定位 divorce">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-069" data-mark-label="divorce" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="divorce：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="divorce：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=divorce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 divorce" title="在有道查询 divorce">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-069</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.离婚；脱离；v. (与......)离婚；使脱离</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=divorce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>divorce</strong></a>, she remained single and took sole responsibility for the household.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离婚后，她保持单身，独自承担家庭责任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=divorce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>divorce</strong></a> left him single, with sole ownership of the small flat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离婚后，他恢复单身，并独自拥有那间小公寓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A single parent may have sole custody following a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=divorce&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>divorce</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">单亲家长可能在离婚后拥有单独监护权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">file for divorce</span> · 提出离婚</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=custody&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">custody</a></span> · 监护权</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=independence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">independence</a></span> · 独立</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-070">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-070" class="vocabulary-word-title">single<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-070" aria-label="定位 single">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-070" data-mark-label="single" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="single：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="single：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=single&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 single" title="在有道查询 single">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-070</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">adj.单一的；单身的</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">After the divorce, she remained <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=single&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>single</strong></a> and took sole responsibility for the household.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离婚后，她保持单身，独自承担家庭责任。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The divorce left him <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=single&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>single</strong></a>, with sole ownership of the small flat.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">离婚后，他恢复单身，并独自拥有那间小公寓。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=single&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>single</strong></a> parent may have sole custody following a divorce.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">单亲家长可能在离婚后拥有单独监护权。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">remain single</span> · 保持单身</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=custody&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">custody</a></span> · 监护权</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=independence&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">independence</a></span> · 独立</li></ul>
</div>
</details>
