### 词群 19.07｜社区与工作中的关系 {#group-19-07}

**串联方式：** 由通勤者、邻里和成员进入上下级、伙伴和同事，再比较熟人、朋友与陌生人。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-075">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-075" class="vocabulary-word-title">commuter<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-075" aria-label="定位 commuter">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-075" data-mark-label="commuter" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="commuter：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="commuter：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commuter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 commuter" title="在有道查询 commuter">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-075</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commuter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commuter</strong></a> who recently became a club member helped organise a neighbourhood event.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位最近加入俱乐部的通勤者帮助组织了社区活动。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Each neighbourhood member can complete a survey about <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commuter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commuter</strong></a> services.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">每位社区成员都可填写通勤服务调查。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commuter&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commuter</strong></a> met another member of the neighbourhood association on the morning train.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位通勤者在早班火车上遇见邻里协会的另一位成员。</p></li>
</ol>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The commuter met another member of the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=neighbourhood&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>neighbourhood</strong></a> association on the morning train.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位通勤者在早班火车上遇见邻里协会的另一位成员。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-078">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-078" class="vocabulary-word-title">boss<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-078" aria-label="定位 boss">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-078" data-mark-label="boss" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="boss：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="boss：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 boss" title="在有道查询 boss">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-078</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.老板，上司</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boss</strong></a> asked the manager to support the supervisor and listen to every subordinate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老板要求经理支持主管，并倾听每位下属。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A subordinate may report a concern to the supervisor, manager or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boss</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下属可向主管、经理或老板反映问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The manager warned the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=boss&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>boss</strong></a> that a supervisor had treated a subordinate unfairly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理向老板报告，一位主管不公平地对待了下属。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a supportive boss</span> · 支持员工的老板</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reporting%20line&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reporting line</a></span> · 汇报关系</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workplace%20culture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">workplace culture</a></span> · 职场文化</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-079">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The boss asked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manager&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manager</strong></a> to support the supervisor and listen to every subordinate.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">老板要求经理支持主管，并倾听每位下属。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A subordinate may report a concern to the supervisor, <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manager&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manager</strong></a> or boss.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">下属可向主管、经理或老板反映问题。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=manager&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>manager</strong></a> warned the boss that a supervisor had treated a subordinate unfairly.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">经理向老板报告，一位主管不公平地对待了下属。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a project manager</span> · 项目经理</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=reporting%20line&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">reporting line</a></span> · 汇报关系</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=workplace%20culture&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">workplace culture</a></span> · 职场文化</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-082">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-082" class="vocabulary-word-title">partner<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-082" aria-label="定位 partner">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-082" data-mark-label="partner" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="partner：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="partner：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=partner&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 partner" title="在有道查询 partner">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-082</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.伙伴，盟友；</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">My colleague introduced a business <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=partner&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>partner</strong></a>, an old school mate, a political comrade and a casual acquaintance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">同事介绍了一位商业伙伴、老同学、政治同志及普通熟人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A chance acquaintance became a colleague, then a business <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=partner&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>partner</strong></a>, while an old mate remained a trusted comrade.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位偶然认识的人先成了同事，再成为商业伙伴，而老朋友仍是可信战友。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memoir distinguishes a professional <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=partner&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>partner</strong></a> from a colleague, a close mate, a political comrade and a distant acquaintance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回忆录区分了职业伙伴、同事、密友、政治同志与疏远熟人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a business partner</span> · 商业伙伴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=social%20network&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">social network</a></span> · 人际网络</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trust</a></span> · 信任</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">My colleague introduced a business partner, an old school <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mate</strong></a>, a political comrade and a casual acquaintance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">同事介绍了一位商业伙伴、老同学、政治同志及普通熟人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A chance acquaintance became a colleague, then a business partner, while an old <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mate</strong></a> remained a trusted comrade.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位偶然认识的人先成了同事，再成为商业伙伴，而老朋友仍是可信战友。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memoir distinguishes a professional partner from a colleague, a close <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mate&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>mate</strong></a>, a political comrade and a distant acquaintance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回忆录区分了职业伙伴、同事、密友、政治同志与疏远熟人。</p></li>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A chance acquaintance became a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=colleague&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>colleague</strong></a>, then a business partner, while an old mate remained a trusted comrade.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位偶然认识的人先成了同事，再成为商业伙伴，而老朋友仍是可信战友。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-085">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-085" class="vocabulary-word-title">comrade<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-085" aria-label="定位 comrade">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-085" data-mark-label="comrade" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="comrade：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="comrade：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comrade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 comrade" title="在有道查询 comrade">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-085</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.同志；(尤指共患难的)同伴</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">My colleague introduced a business partner, an old school mate, a political <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comrade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>comrade</strong></a> and a casual acquaintance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">同事介绍了一位商业伙伴、老同学、政治同志及普通熟人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A chance acquaintance became a colleague, then a business partner, while an old mate remained a trusted <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comrade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>comrade</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位偶然认识的人先成了同事，再成为商业伙伴，而老朋友仍是可信战友。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The memoir distinguishes a professional partner from a colleague, a close mate, a political <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=comrade&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>comrade</strong></a> and a distant acquaintance.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">回忆录区分了职业伙伴、同事、密友、政治同志与疏远熟人。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a political comrade</span> · 政治同志</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=social%20network&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">social network</a></span> · 人际网络</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=trust&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">trust</a></span> · 信任</li></ul>
</div>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-087">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-087" class="vocabulary-word-title">friendship<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-087" aria-label="定位 friendship">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-087" data-mark-label="friendship" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="friendship：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="friendship：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=friendship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 friendship" title="在有道查询 friendship">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-087</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.友谊；友情，友好</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A stranger became a friend, and their <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=friendship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>friendship</strong></a> grew through shared affection for music.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">陌生人成了朋友，他们因共同热爱音乐而加深友谊。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Small acts of kindness can turn a stranger into someone whose <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=friendship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>friendship</strong></a> and affection matter deeply.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">微小善举能把陌生人变成其友谊与感情令人珍视的人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel traces a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=friendship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>friendship</strong></a> from the first meeting with a stranger to a lifetime of mutual affection.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说追踪一段友谊，从初遇陌生人到终生相互关爱。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a lasting friendship</span> · 持久友谊</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=companionship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">companionship</a></span> · 陪伴</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=kindness&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">kindness</a></span> · 善意</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-088">

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Small acts of kindness can turn a stranger into someone whose friendship and <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affection</strong></a> matter deeply.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">微小善举能把陌生人变成其友谊与感情令人珍视的人。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The novel traces a friendship from the first meeting with a stranger to a lifetime of mutual <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=affection&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>affection</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">小说追踪一段友谊，从初遇陌生人到终生相互关爱。</p></li>

### 词群 19.08｜组织里的专业成长与领导 {#group-19-08}

**串联方式：** 由学徒、副手进入领导与支持者，再看委员会和理事会；hero、actress 是人物与表演身份。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-090">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-090" class="vocabulary-word-title">apprentice<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-090" aria-label="定位 apprentice">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-090" data-mark-label="apprentice" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="apprentice：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="apprentice：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apprentice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 apprentice" title="在有道查询 apprentice">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-090</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.学徒，徒弟；v.使当......的学徒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The deputy arranged training for the new <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apprentice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>apprentice</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">副主任为新学徒安排了培训。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">As an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apprentice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>apprentice</strong></a>, she never expected to become the director's deputy.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">当学徒时，她从未想过自己会成为主任的副手。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The deputy praised the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=apprentice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>apprentice</strong></a> for identifying a safety problem.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">副主管赞扬学徒发现了安全问题。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a skilled apprentice</span> · 熟练的学徒</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=mentorship&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">mentorship</a></span> · 导师指导</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=promotion&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">promotion</a></span> · 晋升</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-091">

<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.男主角，男主人公；英雄；勇士</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actress plays a heroine who saves the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hero&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hero</strong></a> from danger.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位演员扮演把男主角从危险中救出的女主角。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director asked the actress to portray the heroine as resourceful as the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hero&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hero</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">导演要求演员把女主角演得与男主角同样机智。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The actress argued that the heroine deserved as much attention as the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hero&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hero</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">这位演员认为女主角应得到与男主角一样多的关注。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a national hero</span> · 民族英雄</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=character&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">character</a></span> · 角色</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=performance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">performance</a></span> · 表演</li></ul>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-095">

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n. (德国或奥地利)总理；(英国)财政大臣</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chancellor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chancellor</strong></a> invited a former commander and a company director to speak at the university.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">校监邀请一位前指挥官和一位公司董事到大学演讲。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A museum director consulted the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chancellor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chancellor</strong></a> and the commander about a joint exhibition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆馆长向校监和指挥官咨询联合展览。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The director thanked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=chancellor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>chancellor</strong></a> for hosting the retired commander at the ceremony.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">主任感谢校监在典礼上接待退役指挥官。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a university chancellor</span> · 大学校监</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=leadership&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">leadership</a></span> · 领导</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=public%20service&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">public service</a></span> · 公共服务</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A museum director consulted the chancellor and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=commander&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>commander</strong></a> about a joint exhibition.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">博物馆馆长向校监和指挥官咨询联合展览。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-098">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-098" class="vocabulary-word-title">proponent<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-098" aria-label="定位 proponent">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-098" data-mark-label="proponent" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="proponent：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="proponent：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 proponent" title="在有道查询 proponent">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-098</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.拥护者，支持者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proponent</strong></a> of the scheme asked the committee to recommend it to the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该方案的一位倡导者请委员会向议会推荐它。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council appointed a committee that included a prominent <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proponent</strong></a> of public transport.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议会任命了一个委员会，其中包括一位知名公共交通倡导者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The committee heard from a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=proponent&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>proponent</strong></a> before presenting its findings to the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">委员会先听取一位倡导者意见，再向议会提交结论。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a leading proponent</span> · 主要倡导者</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=governance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">governance</a></span> · 治理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consultation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consultation</a></span> · 咨询</li></ul>

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A proponent of the scheme asked the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=committee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>committee</strong></a> to recommend it to the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该方案的一位倡导者请委员会向议会推荐它。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The council appointed a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=committee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>committee</strong></a> that included a prominent proponent of public transport.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议会任命了一个委员会，其中包括一位知名公共交通倡导者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=committee&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>committee</strong></a> heard from a proponent before presenting its findings to the council.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">委员会先听取一位倡导者意见，再向议会提交结论。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a planning committee</span> · 规划委员会</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=governance&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">governance</a></span> · 治理</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=consultation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">consultation</a></span> · 咨询</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-100">

<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A proponent of the scheme asked the committee to recommend it to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=council&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>council</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">该方案的一位倡导者请委员会向议会推荐它。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=council&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>council</strong></a> appointed a committee that included a prominent proponent of public transport.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">议会任命了一个委员会，其中包括一位知名公共交通倡导者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The committee heard from a proponent before presenting its findings to the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=council&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>council</strong></a>.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">委员会先听取一位倡导者意见，再向议会提交结论。</p></li>
</ol>

### 词群 19.09｜提供专业意见与裁决的人 {#group-19-09}

**串联方式：** 辨认专家、顾问、咨询师、律师和裁判；veterinary 本身为形容词，兽医可用 veterinarian。

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-101">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-101" class="vocabulary-word-title">specialist<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-101" aria-label="定位 specialist">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-101" data-mark-label="specialist" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="specialist：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="specialist：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 specialist" title="在有道查询 specialist">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-101</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.专家；专科医生</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The careers fair featured a veterinary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>specialist</strong></a>, a business consultant and a school counsellor.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">职业展邀请了兽医专家、商业顾问和学校辅导员。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A counsellor helped the veterinary <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>specialist</strong></a> find a consultant to advise on opening a clinic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辅导员帮助兽医专家找到一位为开诊所提供建议的顾问。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The veterinary course invited a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=specialist&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>specialist</strong></a> to discuss animal health, a consultant to discuss business and a counsellor to discuss stress.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">兽医课程邀请专家讨论动物健康、顾问讨论商业，并请辅导员讨论压力。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a medical specialist</span> · 医学专家</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=professional%20advice&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">professional advice</a></span> · 专业建议</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=career%20development&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">career development</a></span> · 职业发展</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=counsellor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>counsellor</strong></a> helped the veterinary specialist find a consultant to advise on opening a clinic.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">辅导员帮助兽医专家找到一位为开诊所提供建议的顾问。</p></li>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-105">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-105" class="vocabulary-word-title">solicitor<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-105" aria-label="定位 solicitor">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-105" data-mark-label="solicitor" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="solicitor：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="solicitor：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solicitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 solicitor" title="在有道查询 solicitor">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-105</span></span></summary>
<div class="vocabulary-word-content">
<p class="vocabulary-meaning">n.(英国的)诉状律师，事务律师；（美国的)法务官</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solicitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solicitor</strong></a> and the attorney asked an umpire to explain the disputed match rules.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">两位律师请裁判解释有争议的比赛规则。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike an umpire who decides during a game, a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solicitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solicitor</strong></a> or attorney may spend months preparing a legal case.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与比赛中当场判定的裁判不同，律师可能花数月准备案件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The umpire consulted a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=solicitor&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>solicitor</strong></a> after receiving a claim from an attorney representing the club.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">裁判收到代表俱乐部的律师的索赔要求后，咨询了一位律师。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a family solicitor</span> · 家庭事务律师</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adjudication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adjudication</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dispute</a></span> · 争端</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-106">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The solicitor and the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attorney&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attorney</strong></a> asked an umpire to explain the disputed match rules.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">两位律师请裁判解释有争议的比赛规则。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike an umpire who decides during a game, a solicitor or <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attorney&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attorney</strong></a> may spend months preparing a legal case.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与比赛中当场判定的裁判不同，律师可能花数月准备案件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The umpire consulted a solicitor after receiving a claim from an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=attorney&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>attorney</strong></a> representing the club.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">裁判收到代表俱乐部的律师的索赔要求后，咨询了一位律师。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">an experienced attorney</span> · 经验丰富的律师</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adjudication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adjudication</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dispute</a></span> · 争端</li></ul>
</div>
</details>

<details class="custom-block details vocabulary-word" data-word-id="19-107">
<summary><h4 id="word-19-107" class="vocabulary-word-title">umpire<a class="header-anchor" href="#word-19-107" aria-label="定位 umpire">&#8203;</a></h4><button type="button" class="vocabulary-word-mark" data-mark-word="19-107" data-mark-label="umpire" aria-pressed="false" aria-label="umpire：红色，点击切换为绿色" title="umpire：红色，点击切换为绿色">🔴</button><span class="vocabulary-word-tools"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link vocabulary-word-dictionary no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=umpire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer" aria-label="在有道查询 umpire" title="在有道查询 umpire">🌐</a><span class="vocabulary-word-id">19-107</span></span></summary>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The solicitor and the attorney asked an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=umpire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>umpire</strong></a> to explain the disputed match rules.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">两位律师请裁判解释有争议的比赛规则。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">Unlike an <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=umpire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>umpire</strong></a> who decides during a game, a solicitor or attorney may spend months preparing a legal case.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">与比赛中当场判定的裁判不同，律师可能花数月准备案件。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=umpire&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>umpire</strong></a> consulted a solicitor after receiving a claim from an attorney representing the club.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">裁判收到代表俱乐部的律师的索赔要求后，咨询了一位律师。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">a cricket umpire</span> · 板球裁判</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=adjudication&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">adjudication</a></span> · 裁决</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=dispute&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">dispute</a></span> · 争端</li></ul>
</div>

### 词群 19.10｜日常服务、劳动与弱势处境 {#group-19-10}

**串联方式：** 从收银、消防、护理、园艺等服务进入劳动，再讨论被挟持、乞讨与奴役处境；haircut 是服务项目。

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cashier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cashier</strong></a>, fireman, nurse and gardener all volunteered at the community fair.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">收银员、消防员、护士和园丁都在社区集市做志愿者。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A nurse treated the gardener while a fireman checked the building and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=cashier&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>cashier</strong></a> helped customers leave.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">护士照顾园丁，消防员检查建筑，收银员帮助顾客离开。</p></li>

</ol>

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The groom visited the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barber</strong></a> for a haircut before meeting his father, a fisherman, and his uncle, a butcher.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">新郎去理发师处理发，随后见了当渔民的父亲和当肉贩的叔叔。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The village <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barber</strong></a> gave the groom a haircut while the fisherman and butcher prepared for the wedding.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">渔民和肉贩准备婚礼时，村里的理发师为新郎理发。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A fisherman recommended the <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=barber&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>barber</strong></a> whose haircut had impressed the butcher when he was a groom.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位渔民推荐了那位理发师；肉贩当新郎时，对他的理发手艺印象深刻。</p></li>
</ol>

<div class="vocabulary-word-content">

<p class="vocabulary-label">例句</p>
<ol class="vocabulary-examples">
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical novel follows a slave, a beggar and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostage</strong></a> whose lives are shaped by poverty and toil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史小说描写奴隶、乞丐与人质，他们的生活受到贫穷和艰辛劳动的影响。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A former <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostage</strong></a> described the toil endured by a slave and the hardship faced by a beggar in the same town.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位前人质描述了同镇奴隶忍受的苦役与乞丐面临的困苦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition uses a slave's testimony, a beggar's diary and a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=hostage&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>hostage</strong></a>'s letter to reveal lives of toil and insecurity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览用奴隶证言、乞丐日记和人质书信展示辛劳而缺乏保障的生活。</p></li>
</ol>
<p class="vocabulary-label">常用搭配</p><p class="vocabulary-collocation"><span lang="en">free a hostage</span> · 释放人质</p>
<p class="vocabulary-label">联想词</p><ul class="vocabulary-related"><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=exploitation&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">exploitation</a></span> · 剥削</li><li><span class="vocabulary-relation">同场景</span> <span lang="en"><a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=vulnerability&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer">vulnerability</a></span> · 弱势处境</li></ul>

<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The historical novel follows a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slave</strong></a>, a beggar and a hostage whose lives are shaped by poverty and toil.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">历史小说描写奴隶、乞丐与人质，他们的生活受到贫穷和艰辛劳动的影响。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">A former hostage described the toil endured by a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slave</strong></a> and the hardship faced by a beggar in the same town.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">一位前人质描述了同镇奴隶忍受的苦役与乞丐面临的困苦。</p></li>
<li><p class="vocabulary-example-en" lang="en">The exhibition uses a <a class="vocabulary-dictionary-link no-icon" href="https://youdao.com/result?word=slave&amp;lang=en" target="_blank" rel="noopener noreferrer"><strong>slave</strong></a>'s testimony, a beggar's diary and a hostage's letter to reveal lives of toil and insecurity.</p><p class="vocabulary-example-zh" lang="zh-CN">展览用奴隶证言、乞丐日记和人质书信展示辛劳而缺乏保障的生活。</p></li>
</ol>

### 本章 Language Takeaway

优先尝试主动说出的三个表达：

- **share household chores**：分担家务；share 在第 16 章出现。
- **develop a friendship**：发展友谊。
- **consult a specialist**：咨询专家；consult 在第 5 章出现。

### 串学示范｜自拟短文

A person may be a sibling at home, a colleague at work and a customer in a shop. Each role brings different expectations. Family members may share household chores, while a manager coordinates a team. When specialist help is needed, people can consult a counsellor, solicitor or another professional.

**中文理解：** 一个人在家中可能是兄弟姐妹，在工作中是同事，在商店里是顾客。不同角色伴随不同期待。家庭成员可以分担家务，管理者则协调团队。需要专业帮助时，人们可以咨询顾问、律师或其他专业人士。

### 闭卷回忆｜先尝试，再逐级提示

**任务：** 从家庭、工作和服务场景各选一个角色，说清人与人的关系。

1. 关掉本章正文，在空白页列出你记得的词群；每群先写 3—5 个英文词或搭配。
2. 从中选 3 个词群，连成 3—5 句英文；用明确关系连接，专业分类和事实不确定时先核对。
3. 卡住时，先只看“本章主线”；还想不起，再看词群标题和中文简释，最后才看英文。
4. 用核对编号找出遗漏词；把“见到认得”与“想表达时能说出”分开记录。
5. 合上提示重新说一遍；下次换一个对象、地点或例子，检查能否迁移。

可直接复制的空白页：

```text
主题：社会角色
我记得的词群：
每群想起的词或搭配：
今天的 3 个 takeaway：
我的 3—5 句英文：
看提示后才想起的核对编号：
下次换什么情境再说：
```
